Which sewers shall be owned and controlled by said Town; providing the term of said bonds, the rate of interest to be paid thereon, when such interest shall be paid and the dates of maturity thereof ; ordering a special election for the purpose of submitting to the taxpayers affected thereby the question of incurring said indebtedness and issuing said bonds; fixing the details of said election; repealing all ordinances, orders or resolutions or parts thereof in conflict herewith and declaring an emergency
